Memory System Evaluator

Ten memory systems and two baselines on one model, one embedder, one answer prompt, one calibrated judge. Every number below links to the question, the retrieved context, the answer and the judge's reasoning that produced it. Round 1 (feasibility, S1: 90 memories, 25 questions) — 11/12 systems ran.

S1 has a ~7pp inter-run noise envelope (RESULTS_SUMMARY §1): differences inside it are ties. Round 1 decides feasibility and cost class, not ranking; round 2 (SixAxis, 3 replications, 2 model tiers) ranks.

Round 2 — SixAxis (pre-registered: 3 seeds × generator tiers, strict-judge v3 pinned to Sonnet)

Mean ± std across seeds. A1 recall · A2 update (FAMA = current − stale) · A3 deletion · A4 adversarial · A5 cost · judge health = decoy acceptance (should be ~0). GraphRAG runs as a context builder with the shared answer prompt in this round.
